From the dialogue so far and your candidates from the previous turn, list the disorders this patient could have.\n\n[Current Turn]\n16\n\n[Previous Candidates]\nF20.9, F22.9, F25.9\n\n[Dialogue History]\nDoctor: Hello, welcome to the clinic. Can you tell me what brings you in today?\n\nPatient: I’m here because… I’ve been hearing Garrett’s voice. Like he’s right there behind me, on the trail or in my painting room. I know he’s dead, at least part of me knows that, but when it happens it feels completely real.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever seen things that others don't see, or believed something strongly that others around you don't seem to understand or agree with?\n\nPatient: Yes. Mostly I hear him—Garrett. His voice, like he’s right behind me, on the trail or in my painting room. I know he’s… gone, but when it’s happening, it doesn’t feel like that. It feels real.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever seen things that others don't see, or believed something strongly that others around you don't seem to understand or agree with?\n\nPatient: Yes… like I said, it’s mostly Garrett’s voice. I’ll be on the trail, or in my painting room, and it’s as clear as if he’s standing right behind me. I know he’s dead, but when it happens… it doesn’t feel that way. It feels real.\n\nDoctor: Besides hearing Garrett’s voice, have you ever seen things that others don't see, or believed something strongly that others around you don't seem to understand or agree with?\n\nPatient: Not really seeing things, no. It’s more… hearing him. Garrett’s voice. Sometimes it’s so clear, like he’s right behind me on the trail or in my painting room. I know he’s dead, but in that moment it feels real.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any experiences where you see someone or something that others don’t, or believe something that you know other people might not think is true?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I mean, I don’t really see things. It’s more that I hear Garrett. His voice can be so clear, like he’s right behind me on the trail or in my painting room. I know he’s dead, but when it’s happening, it feels… real.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any beliefs or thoughts that you know are not shared by others, or that you've come to believe strongly in even though others might not agree with you?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so. Not beliefs, exactly. I mean… people don’t always understand how real it feels to me sometimes, but I wouldn’t say I have some belief everyone disagrees with.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had thoughts or beliefs that you knew were very strange or not based in reality, even if other people didn’t agree with you?\n\nPatient: No… I don’t think so. Not in that way. I mean, I’ve had things feel real in the moment, but I wouldn’t say I knew I was believing something strange or… outside reality.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any thoughts or ideas that you strongly believed were true at the time, even though they didn’t turn out to be based on reality?\n\nPatient: I… no, I don’t think so. Nothing comes to mind like that. I’ve had moments where things felt confusing, but not a belief I was sure of and then later found out wasn’t real.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a strong belief or idea that you were completely sure was true at the time, even if it turned out not to be real or made no sense later?\n\nPatient: I don’t think I’ve had that. I mean… there have been times things felt intense or hard to sort out, but I can’t point to some belief I was completely sure of that later made no sense.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any experiences where you believed something strongly, even if it didn’t turn out to be true, like someone was following you, sending you secret messages, or had special importance to you? Even if just at the time?\n\nPatient: No… I don’t think so. Not like being followed or messages or anything. I mean, I’ve felt things were… important in the moment, maybe, but I can’t say there was a specific belief like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever believed something strongly that turned out not to be true, even if just for a period of time—like someone was trying to harm you, or that you had a special ability or connection to something others didn’t?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure. Not that I can clearly say. I know I’ve gotten caught up in things feeling very meaningful, but… believing I had some special ability or that someone was trying to hurt me, no, I don’t think so.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a time when you believed something strongly, even if just for a while, that other people didn’t seem to understand or didn’t agree with, like something was meant for you specifically, or that you had a special connection to something others didn’t?\n\nPatient: I don’t know. I don’t really think so. Maybe I’ve felt like things had meaning at the time, but not… not in a way I can clearly say was meant for me or some special connection.\n\nDoctor: Have there been times when you believed something was real or true, even though others told you it wasn’t, and it wasn’t just about hearing Garrett’s voice?\n\nPatient: Not really… no. I mean, aside from that, I can’t think of anything I believed like that. I know he’s dead, but when it’s happening, it feels… real.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any beliefs or thoughts that you know are not shared by others, or that you've come to believe strongly in even though others might not agree with you?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so.     "traumatic_stressor": null,

    "additional_requirements": [
      "Absence of prominent hallucinations other than auditory type",
      "No evidence of disorganized thinking, grossly disorganized behavior, or negative symptoms",
      "Absence of a major mood episode concurrent with the delusional periods"
    ]
  }
}
